# configuration file /etc/nginx/nginx.conf:
# ===============================================================
# NGINX TURBO — NET SHIELD IPTV ready (2026-04-20)
# Capacity target: 500+ concurrent streams, 40k connections max
# ===============================================================
user www-data;
worker_processes auto;
worker_rlimit_nofile 200000;
worker_shutdown_timeout 30s;
pid /run/nginx.pid;
include /etc/nginx/modules-enabled/*.conf;

thread_pool iptv threads=32 max_queue=65536;

events {
    worker_connections 20000;
    use epoll;
    multi_accept on;
    accept_mutex off;
}

http {
    lua_package_path "/etc/nginx/lua/?.lua;/usr/local/share/lua/5.1/?.lua;/usr/share/lua/5.1/?.lua;;";
    # Basic
    sendfile on;
    tcp_nopush on;
    tcp_nodelay on;
    server_tokens off;
    types_hash_max_size 2048;
    server_names_hash_bucket_size 128;
    
    include /etc/nginx/mime.types;
    default_type application/octet-stream;

    # Keepalive agresivo (IPTV reuses connections)
    keepalive_timeout 65;
    keepalive_requests 100000;
    reset_timedout_connection on;

    # Client buffers para listas 300+ MB
    client_body_buffer_size 128k;
    client_header_buffer_size 4k;
    large_client_header_buffers 8 16k;
    client_max_body_size 600M;
    client_body_timeout 120s;
    client_header_timeout 60s;
    send_timeout 180s;

    # Output buffers (streaming friendly)
    output_buffers 8 256k;
    postpone_output 1460;

    # Open file cache (crucial para listas cached)
    open_file_cache max=200000 inactive=30s;
    open_file_cache_valid 60s;
    open_file_cache_min_uses 1;
    open_file_cache_errors on;

    # SSL tuning (TLS reuse = menos CPU)
    ssl_session_cache shared:SSL:50m;
    ssl_session_timeout 1d;
    ssl_session_tickets on;
    ssl_protocols TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;
    ssl_prefer_server_ciphers off;
    ssl_buffer_size 8k;

    # Gzip (para manifests y JSON)
    gzip on;
    gzip_vary on;
    gzip_proxied any;
    gzip_comp_level 4;
    gzip_min_length 1024;
    gzip_types application/vnd.apple.mpegurl application/x-mpegurl application/json application/javascript text/css text/plain text/xml;
    gzip_static on;

    # Logs
    log_format iptv '$remote_addr $upstream_cache_status [$time_iso8601] "$request" $status $body_bytes_sent $request_time';
    access_log /var/log/nginx/access.log;
    error_log /var/log/nginx/error.log warn;

    # Proxy cache para IPTV segments
    proxy_cache_path /dev/shm/nginx_cache
        levels=1:2
        keys_zone=iptv_cache:50m
        max_size=500m
        inactive=2h
        use_temp_path=off
        loader_threshold=1000
        loader_files=2000;

    # Upstream hosts keepalive (reduce handshake overhead al provider)

    # Include virtual hosts
    include /etc/nginx/conf.d/*.conf;
    include /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/*;
}

# configuration file /etc/nginx/conf.d/00-ape-manifest-nocache.conf:
# Manifests (.m3u8) NUNCA cacheados -> siempre live (no freeze). Segmentos conservan su cache 45s (anti-403).
map $uri $ape_manifest_nocache {
    default      0;
    "~*\.m3u8$"  1;
}

# configuration file /etc/nginx/conf.d/00-ape-match-zone.conf:
# APE MATCH zone — 1 req por zap; burst cubre zapping rápido
limit_req_zone $binary_remote_addr zone=ape_match:1m rate=120r/m;

# configuration file /etc/nginx/conf.d/00-ape-pull-zone.conf:
# APE outbound pull rate-limit zone (sec review HIGH#3) — aislado, rollback: rm este archivo
limit_req_zone $binary_remote_addr zone=ape_pull:1m rate=30r/m;

# configuration file /etc/nginx/conf.d/50-ape-meta-plane.conf:
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# APE META-PLANE v2 — metadata-only edge + módulos Lua integrados
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# v1 (2026-10-24 15:32): rebase de URIs + candado de video. Validado.
# v2 (misma fecha): integra los módulos que estaban sueltos, SIN que se estorben:
#
#   fase          | módulo                      | por qué no interfiere
#   --------------|-----------------------------|------------------------------------
#   header_filter | sentinel_auth_guard         | observador passthrough (pcall, sin
#                 |                             | ngx.exit): solo cuenta 401/403/5xx y
#                 |                             | añade X-APE-Sentinel
#   log (después  | ape_wake_on_manifest        | corre DESPUÉS de enviar la respuesta
#   de responder) |                             | → 0 impacto en TTFB; encola wake
#   body_filter   | ape_uri_rebase              | único body filter del location (nginx
#                 |                             | admite uno por location)
#
#   librerías cableadas por require (no son handlers), ejercitadas por
#   /_ape/luatest:  bw_adaptive_floor · ape_profile_resolver
#
#   NO se cablean (con motivo documentado en docs/):
#     shield_follow_302  -> requiere lua-resty-http, AUSENTE en el VPS (no hay apt
#                           ni luarocks). Cablearlo rompería la petición.
#     follow_redirect    -> incompleto por diseño: usa ngx.say en fase de
#                           cabeceras y necesita un location /_follow_redirect que
#                           no existe. Además: los players siguen 302 nativamente,
#                           seguirlo en servidor duplica peticiones y latencia.
#
# Decisión de Content-Length: al reescribir el cuerpo cambia el tamaño, así que
# se oculta el del upstream (`proxy_hide_header`) → nginx responde chunked. Esto
# libera la fase header_filter (una sola directiva *_by_lua por fase) para el
# sentinel, sin conflicto.
#
# ESTADO: escucha en :8088 (no toca el vhost público del 443).
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# ── shared dicts (contexto http) ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# circuit_metrics: lo usan sentinel_auth_guard y bandwidth_reactor/combined_body_filter
lua_shared_dict circuit_metrics 10m;
# lab_config: cache del profile map + config del LAB (ape_profile_resolver, lab_config)
lua_shared_dict lab_config 1m;
# ape_flags: interruptores en caliente del plano
lua_shared_dict ape_flags 1m;
# ape_qoe: telemetría del plano (contadores reales, los publica /_ape/qoe)
lua_shared_dict ape_qoe 1m;
# ape_hosts: circuit-breaker suave de CDNs (host caído = 15 s fuera)
lua_shared_dict ape_hosts 1m;

# NOTA: `lua_package_path` NO se declara aquí. El nginx.conf del VPS ya lo define
# globalmente (línea 22): "/etc/nginx/lua/?.lua;...". Declararlo otra vez provoca
# "lua_package_path directive is duplicate" y nginx se niega a recargar
# (verificado: el gate nginx -t abortó y el install hizo rollback limpio).

resolver 127.0.0.1:53 valid=60s ipv6=off;
resolver_timeout 3s;

server {
    listen 8088;
    server_name _;

    # Directorio de módulos Lua (remapeable por sed en los tests aislados)
    set $ape_lua_dir "/etc/nginx/lua/";

    # ── plano de metadata: playlists ─────────────────────────────────────────
    location ~ ^/meta/(?<ape_host>[A-Za-z0-9._-]+)(?<ape_path>/.*)$ {
        set $ape_upstream_origin "http://$ape_host";

        proxy_pass              http://$ape_host$ape_path$is_args$args;
        proxy_http_version      1.1;
        proxy_set_header        Host $ape_host;
        proxy_set_header        Connection "";
        proxy_set_header        User-Agent "Mozilla/5.0";
        proxy_pass_request_headers off;

        proxy_buffering         on;
        proxy_connect_timeout   3s;
        proxy_read_timeout      60s;
        proxy_send_timeout      5s;
        proxy_next_upstream     error timeout;
        proxy_cache             off;

        # El cuerpo se reescribe → el Content-Length del upstream ya no vale.
        # Observabilidad passthrough (nunca bloquea, nunca ngx.exit)
        header_filter_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_header.lua;

        # Rebase de URIs a absolutas del proveedor (corazón del plano)
        body_filter_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_uri_rebase.lua;

        # Wake-on-manifest: post-respuesta, no afecta TTFB ni reproducción
        log_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_wake_on_manifest.lua;

        add_header X-APE-Mode "metadata-only" always;
        add_header X-APE-Meta-Mode "rebase-only" always;
        add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
    }

    # ── tests de integración (LuaJIT real, solo loopback) ───────────────────
    location = /_ape/luatest {
        allow 127.0.0.1;
        deny all;
        content_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_luatest.lua;
    }

    # ── flags en caliente (solo loopback) ───────────────────────────────────
    # GET /_ape/flags            -> lista
    # GET /_ape/flags?k=x&v=1    -> set
    location = /_ape/flags {
        allow 127.0.0.1;
        deny all;
        content_by_lua_block {
            local cjson = require "cjson.safe"
            local d = ngx.shared.ape_flags
            local k = ngx.var.arg_k
            if k and k ~= "" then
                local v = ngx.var.arg_v
                if v == nil then d:delete(k) else d:set(k, v) end
            end
            local out = {}
            local defaults = {
                rebase = "1", enrich = "0", virtual4k = "0", wake = "1", sentinel = "1",
                auth = "0", hint = "0",
                -- DECISIÓN DEL PROPIETARIO 2026-10-24: 4K prevalece; 4K real -> 8K.
                uhd_auto = "1", uhd4k = "1", uhd8k = "0", uhd_pq = "0",
            }
            for k, dv in pairs(defaults) do
                local v = d:get(k)
                out[k] = v or dv
            end
            ngx.header["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
            ngx.say(cjson.encode(out))
        }
    }

    # ── self-test offline del rebase (ruta real: proxy_pass) ────────────────
    location = /__fixture.m3u8 {
        default_type application/vnd.apple.mpegurl;
        alias /etc/nginx/ape-selftest/playlist.m3u8;
    }

    # fixture LL-HLS (con #EXT-X-PART) para probar el passthrough + SERVER-CONTROL
    location = /__fixture_ll.m3u8 {
        default_type application/vnd.apple.mpegurl;
        alias /etc/nginx/ape-selftest/playlist_ll.m3u8;
    }

    # fixture MASTER 1080p AVC (para probar "4K prevalece" sin tocar CODECS)
    location = /__fixture_master.m3u8 {
        default_type application/vnd.apple.mpegurl;
        alias /etc/nginx/ape-selftest/playlist_master.m3u8;
    }

    # fixture MASTER 4K HEVC (para probar "4K -> 8K declarado")
    location = /__fixture_master4k.m3u8 {
        default_type application/vnd.apple.mpegurl;
        alias /etc/nginx/ape-selftest/playlist_master4k.m3u8;
    }

    # self-test completa: sentinel (header) + rebase (body) + wake (log) en UN
    # mismo location → prueba de NO interferencia entre los tres.
    location = /__selftest_full.m3u8 {
        set $ape_upstream_origin "http://provider.test";
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8088/__fixture.m3u8;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host 127.0.0.1;
        header_filter_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_header.lua;
        body_filter_by_lua_file   /etc/nginx/lua/ape_uri_rebase.lua;
        log_by_lua_file           /etc/nginx/lua/ape_wake_on_manifest.lua;
        add_header X-APE-Meta-Mode "rebase-only" always;
    }

    location = /__selftest.m3u8 {
        set $ape_upstream_origin "http://provider.test";
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8088/__fixture.m3u8;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host 127.0.0.1;
        header_filter_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_header.lua;
        body_filter_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_uri_rebase.lua;
    }

    # ── GATEWAY multi-CDN + failover (metadata-only) ────────────────────────
    # /cdn/<host>[:puerto]/<ruta>.m3u8?alt=host2,host3
    # El handler hace failover real, rebase, LL-HLS server-control y QoE.
    location ~ ^/cdn/(?<ape_host>[^/]+)(?<ape_path>/.*)$ {
        content_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_gateway.lua;
    }

    # fetch interno usado por el gateway (subrequest). No expuesto.
    location = /_ape/_fetch {
        internal;
        resolver 127.0.0.1:53 valid=60s ipv6=off;
        # OJO: nginx NO des-escapa las variables $arg_* al usarlas en proxy_pass
        # (medido: "127.0.0.1%3a8089%2f..." -> "could not be resolved"). Por eso el
        # gateway pasa h/p/qs SIN escapar y valida el charset antes de invocar.
        proxy_pass              http://$arg_h$arg_p$arg_qs;
        proxy_http_version      1.1;
        proxy_set_header        Host $arg_h;
        proxy_set_header        Connection "";
        proxy_set_header        User-Agent "Mozilla/5.0";
        proxy_pass_request_headers off;
        proxy_connect_timeout   3s;
        proxy_read_timeout      60s;
        proxy_buffering         on;
    }

    # ── TELEMETRÍA QoE + HEALTH (24/7), solo loopback ───────────────────────
    location = /_ape/qoe {
        allow 127.0.0.1;
        deny all;
        content_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_obs.lua;
    }

    location = /_ape/health {
        allow 127.0.0.1;
        deny all;
        content_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_obs.lua;
    }

    # Puente LAB (Excel → JSON → plano). Solo loopback: es el ADN de config.
    location = /_ape/lab {
        allow 127.0.0.1;
        deny all;
        content_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_lab_api.lua;
    }

    # ── candado: el video no pasa por este plano ────────────────────────────
    location ~* \.(ts|m4s|mp4|m4a|aac|vtt|key)$ {
        return 404;
    }

    location / {
        return 404;
    }
}

# configuration file /etc/nginx/conf.d/iptv-limit-conn.conf:
# ============================================================
# Xtream slot protection - 1 conn simultanea por host:user:pass
# Protege contra 403 max_connections=1 del proveedor Xtream
# ============================================================

# Extrae "host:user:pass" del path shield (solo para manifests .m3u8)
# Si no matches -> vacio -> limit_conn no limita (ej segments .ts, hlsr/)
map $request_uri $xtream_account {
    "~^/shield/[^/]+/([^/]+)/(?:live/)?([^/]+)/([^/]+)/[^/]+\.m3u8"  "$1:$2:$3";
    default                                                            "";
}

# Shared memory zone - 10MB alberga ~150k accounts unicos
limit_conn_zone $xtream_account zone=xtream_slot:10m;

# configuration file /etc/nginx/conf.d/iptv-proxy.conf:
# ===============================================================
# IPTV PROXY CACHE — location /px/ para caching IPTV segments
# Uso: https://iptv-ape.duckdns.org/px/HOST/PATH.ts
# Permite: cualquier player use el VPS como CDN cache de su propio provider
# ===============================================================

map $request_uri $cache_ttl {
    ~*\.m3u8(\?.*)?$  2s;
    ~*\.ts(\?.*)?$    60s;
    ~*\.m4s(\?.*)?$   60s;
    ~*\.aac(\?.*)?$   60s;
    default            0;
}

# configuration file /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/coa-navigator:
# ============================================================================
# COA Navigator PRO — Nginx Reverse Proxy
# ============================================================================
server {
    listen 80;
    server_name _;

    # ── APE PHP scripts (resolve_quality.php, cmaf_proxy.php, etc.) ─────────
    # CRÍTICO: las peticiones internas a 127.0.0.1:80 deben llegar aquí
    root /var/www/html;
    index index.php index.html;

    location ~ \.php$ {
        include snippets/fastcgi-php.conf;
        fastcgi_pass unix:/run/php/php8.3-fpm.sock;
        fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
        fastcgi_read_timeout 30s;
        fastcgi_send_timeout 30s;
        include fastcgi_params;
    }

    # ── COA Navigator PRO (aislado en /coa/) ────────────────────────────────
    location /coa/ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3100/;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_read_timeout 300s;
        proxy_send_timeout 300s;
        proxy_connect_timeout 60s;
        client_max_body_size 50m;
    }

    # ── Socket.IO específico ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    location /coa/socket.io/ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3100/socket.io/;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_read_timeout 86400s;
    }
}

# configuration file /etc/nginx/snippets/fastcgi-php.conf:
# regex to split $uri to $fastcgi_script_name and $fastcgi_path
fastcgi_split_path_info ^(.+?\.php)(/.*)$;

# Check that the PHP script exists before passing it
try_files $fastcgi_script_name =404;

# Bypass the fact that try_files resets $fastcgi_path_info
# see: http://trac.nginx.org/nginx/ticket/321
set $path_info $fastcgi_path_info;
fastcgi_param PATH_INFO $path_info;

fastcgi_index index.php;
include fastcgi.conf;

# configuration file /etc/nginx/fastcgi.conf:

fastcgi_param  SCRIPT_FILENAME    $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
fastcgi_param  QUERY_STRING       $query_string;
fastcgi_param  REQUEST_METHOD     $request_method;
fastcgi_param  CONTENT_TYPE       $content_type;
fastcgi_param  CONTENT_LENGTH     $content_length;

fastcgi_param  SCRIPT_NAME        $fastcgi_script_name;
fastcgi_param  REQUEST_URI        $request_uri;
fastcgi_param  DOCUMENT_URI       $document_uri;
fastcgi_param  DOCUMENT_ROOT      $document_root;
fastcgi_param  SERVER_PROTOCOL    $server_protocol;
fastcgi_param  REQUEST_SCHEME     $scheme;
fastcgi_param  HTTPS              $https if_not_empty;

fastcgi_param  GATEWAY_INTERFACE  CGI/1.1;
fastcgi_param  SERVER_SOFTWARE    nginx/$nginx_version;

fastcgi_param  REMOTE_ADDR        $remote_addr;
fastcgi_param  REMOTE_PORT        $remote_port;
fastcgi_param  REMOTE_USER        $remote_user;

fastcgi_param  SERVER_PORT        $server_port;
fastcgi_param  SERVER_NAME        $server_name;

# PHP only, required if PHP was built with --enable-force-cgi-redirect
fastcgi_param  REDIRECT_STATUS    200;

# configuration file /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/default:
server {
    listen 80 default_server;
    listen [::]:80 default_server;
    server_name iptv-ape.duckdns.org 178.156.147.234;

    # Bypass redirect to HTTPS for the prisma ADB quality API and manifest cache files (for local pull sentinel via nc)
    location ~ ^/prisma/api/prisma-adb-quality\.php$ {
        root /var/www/html;
        include snippets/fastcgi-php.conf;
        fastcgi_pass unix:/run/php/php8.3-fpm.sock;
    }

    location ~ ^/prisma/quality-manifest\.(json|hash)$ {
        root /var/www/html;
        default_type application/json;
    }


    # Everything else redirects to HTTPS
    location / {
        return 301 https://$host$request_uri;
    }
}

server {
    listen 443 ssl default_server;
    listen [::]:443 ssl default_server;
    server_name iptv-ape.duckdns.org 178.156.147.234;

    # SSL Configuration
    ssl_certificate /etc/letsencrypt/live/iptv-ape.duckdns.org/fullchain.pem;
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/iptv-ape.duckdns.org/privkey.pem;
    ssl_protocols TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;
    include snippets/ape-meta-public.conf;

    # VPS SOLO TEXTO: root donde viven las playlists y scripts PHP
    root /var/www/html;

    # TELESCOPE v2.1: Full telemetry aggregation endpoint
    index index.php index.html;

    # Logs dedicados
    access_log /var/log/nginx/playlist_access.log;
    error_log /var/log/nginx/playlist_error.log warn;

    server_tokens off;

    # Performance para texto grande
    sendfile on;
    tcp_nopush on;
    tcp_nodelay on;

    # Timeouts defensivos para uploads grandes
    client_max_body_size 600M;
    client_body_timeout 120s;
    send_timeout 180s;
    keepalive_timeout 30s;

    # Healthcheck
    location = /health {
        add_header Content-Type text/plain;
        return 200 "OK\n";
    }

    # ==============================
    #  PHP UPLOAD SCRIPTS (CHUNKED)
    # ==============================
    location ~ \.php$ {
        # PHP sends its own CORS — only handle preflight OPTIONS here
        if ($request_method = OPTIONS) {
            add_header Access-Control-Allow-Origin "*" always;
            add_header Access-Control-Allow-Methods "GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS" always;
            add_header Access-Control-Allow-Headers "Content-Type, Authorization, X-Upload-Id, X-Chunk-Index, X-Total-Chunks, X-Chunk-SHA256, X-Chunk-MD5, X-Secret-Key, Range, Origin, x-playback-session-id, x-ape-signature, ape-security-token, x-request-profile, X-Prisma-Key" always;
            add_header Access-Control-Max-Age 300 always;
            add_header Content-Type text/plain;
            add_header Content-Length 0;
            return 204;
        }

        # PHP-FPM
        include snippets/fastcgi-php.conf;
        fastcgi_pass unix:/run/php/php8.3-fpm.sock;
        fastcgi_read_timeout 300s;
        fastcgi_send_timeout 300s;
    }


    # ==============================
    #  PLAYLIST M3U8 (GZIP STATIC STREAMING)
    #  500MB+ listas servidas desde .gz pre-comprimido
    #  gunzip descomprime en streaming (16KB chunks, ZERO RAM spike)
    # ==============================
    location ~* \.m3u8$ {
        # MIME correcto
        default_type application/vnd.apple.mpegurl;
        types { application/vnd.apple.mpegurl m3u8; }

        # ═══ GZIP STATIC: Sirve el .gz pre-comprimido directo del disco ═══
        # "always" = sirve .gz incluso si el cliente NO envía Accept-Encoding: gzip
        # En ese caso, gunzip descomprime al vuelo.
        gzip_static always;

        # ═══ GUNZIP: Streaming decompression para clientes legacy ═══
        # Lee el .gz en chunks de 16KB, descomprime y envía sin buffear todo en RAM.
        # Compatible con: OTT Navigator, TiviMate, IPTV Smarters, VLC, Kodi,
        # Perfect Player, GSE, Smart IPTV, y cualquier HTTP client.
        gunzip on;
        
        # Vary para que proxies/CDN cachen ambas versiones
        gzip_vary on;

        # ═══ STREAMING BUFFERS: Controlan el flujo de descompresión ═══
        # 32 buffers de 16KB = 512KB pipeline en memoria (NADA vs 500MB)
        # Nginx lee 16KB del .gz → descomprime → envía al cliente → siguiente chunk
        output_buffers 32 16k;

        # ═══ SENDFILE para el path comprimido (zero-copy kernel) ═══
        sendfile on;
        tcp_nopush on;
        tcp_nodelay on;

        # CORS completo
        add_header Access-Control-Allow-Origin "*" always;
        add_header Access-Control-Allow-Methods "GET, HEAD, OPTIONS" always;
        add_header Access-Control-Allow-Headers "Range, DNT, User-Agent, X-Requested-With, If-Modified-Since, Cache-Control, Content-Type, Accept-Encoding" always;
        add_header Access-Control-Expose-Headers "Content-Length, Content-Range, ETag, Last-Modified, Content-Encoding" always;

        # Preflight (sin redirects; 200 directo)
        if ($request_method = OPTIONS) {
            add_header Content-Type text/plain;
            add_header Content-Length 0;
            return 200;
        }

        # Range requests
        add_header Accept-Ranges bytes always;

        # Cache control — 5 min para que los players re-descarguen periódicamente
        add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=300" always;
        expires 5m;

        # NO REDIRECTS EN PLAYLIST
        limit_except GET HEAD {
            deny all;
        }

        # try_files busca PRIMERO el .gz (gzip_static lo maneja)
        # Si no existe .gz, busca el .m3u8 sin comprimir
        try_files $uri =404;
    }

    # ==============================
    #  STATIC FILES (icons, etc)
    # ==============================
    location / {
        # CORS for other requests
        add_header Access-Control-Allow-Origin "*" always;
        add_header Access-Control-Allow-Methods "GET, POST, OPTIONS" always;
        
        if ($request_method = OPTIONS) {
            add_header Access-Control-Allow-Origin "*" always;
            add_header Access-Control-Allow-Methods "GET, POST, OPTIONS" always;
            add_header Access-Control-Allow-Headers "Content-Type, Authorization, x-playback-session-id, x-ape-signature, ape-security-token, x-request-profile, X-Prisma-Key" always;
            add_header Content-Type text/plain;
            add_header Content-Length 0;
            return 204;
        }
        
        try_files $uri $uri/ =404;
    }

    # ==============================
    #  BLOQUEO: NO VIDEO EN VPS
    # ==============================
    location ~* \.(ts|m4s|mp4)$ {
        return 404;
    }

    # Bloquear archivos sensibles
    location ~* \.(ini|log|sql|bak|env)$ {
        return 404;
    }

    # === APE v3.0 Resolver Security ===\n    location ~ ^/iptv-ape/(logs|cache|modules|profiles|telemetry|mutations)/ {\n        deny all;\n        return 403;\n    }\n
    location ~ /\.ht {
        deny all;
    }
}

# configuration file /etc/nginx/snippets/ape-meta-public.conf:
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# APE META-PUBLIC — locations públicas incluidas DENTRO del server 443 existente
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Se incluye con UNA línea en /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/default:
#     include snippets/ape-meta-public.conf;
# Se hace por include (no creando otro server block) porque un segundo server
# con el mismo server_name NO fusiona locations: gana el primero y el resto se
# ignora silenciosamente.
#
# Seguridad: /cdn/ NO es un proxy abierto. El gateway valida el host contra
# /etc/nginx/ape-hosts-allow.txt (SSRF guard). El trafico de video sigue
# bloqueado (404) porque este plano sólo sirve playlists.
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# Gateway de metadata: /cdn/<host-proveedor>/<ruta>.m3u8[?alt=host2,host3]
location ~ ^/cdn/(?<ape_host>[^/]+)(?<ape_path>/.*)$ {
    # v2 (2026-10-25): el gateway NECESITA /_ape/_fetch, definido SOLO en el
    # server 8088. Ejecutarlo en el 443 devolvia 404 (subrequest a un location
    # inexistente en este contexto) y la lista CDN no reproducia. Ahora el 443
    # delega en el 8088: una sola implementacion y el subrequest en su contexto.
    proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8088;
    proxy_http_version 1.1;
    proxy_set_header Connection "";
    proxy_set_header Host $host;
    proxy_read_timeout 60s;
    proxy_buffering on;
    add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
}

# Health público mínimo (sin métricas internas ni hosts)
location = /ape/health {
    content_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_obs.lua;
    add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
}

# Publicación de la DNA del LAB (solo POST + token)
location = /ape/lab-upload {
    content_by_lua_file /etc/nginx/lua/ape_lab_upload.lua;
    client_max_body_size 2m;
    add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
}

# Candado explícito: NADA de segmentos por el plano público
location ~* \.(ts|m4s|mp4|m4a|aac|vtt|key)$ {
    return 404;
}

# /usr/lib/systemd/system/nginx.service
# Stop dance for nginx
# =======================
#
# ExecStop sends SIGQUIT (graceful stop) to the nginx process.
# If, after 5s (--retry QUIT/5) nginx is still running, systemd takes control
# and sends SIGTERM (fast shutdown) to the main process.
# After another 5s (TimeoutStopSec=5), and if nginx is alive, systemd sends
# SIGKILL to all the remaining processes in the process group (KillMode=mixed).
#
# nginx signals reference doc:
# http://nginx.org/en/docs/control.html
#
[Unit]
Description=A high performance web server and a reverse proxy server
Documentation=man:nginx(8)
After=network-online.target remote-fs.target nss-lookup.target
Wants=network-online.target
ConditionFileIsExecutable=/usr/sbin/nginx

[Service]
Type=forking
PIDFile=/run/nginx.pid
ExecStartPre=/usr/sbin/nginx -t -q -g 'daemon on; master_process on;'
ExecStart=/usr/sbin/nginx -g 'daemon on; master_process on;'
ExecReload=/usr/sbin/nginx -g 'daemon on; master_process on;' -s reload
ExecStop=-/sbin/start-stop-daemon --quiet --stop --retry QUIT/5 --pidfile /run/nginx.pid
TimeoutStopSec=5
KillMode=mixed

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target

# /etc/systemd/system/nginx.service.d/override.conf
[Service]
LimitNOFILE=1048576
LimitNPROC=1048576

# /etc/systemd/system/nginx.service.d/zz-audit-restart.conf
# [APE 2026-10-25] Auditoría de reinicios de nginx (forense, no bloqueante).

#  when session.save_handler=files.
#

#  sessions yourself.
